118	    case 'DontStarveIsUp': return false;
119	    case 'TenthAnniversaryIsUp': return false;
120	    case 'DontStarveIsNotUp': case 'TenthAnniversaryIsNotUp': return true;
121	    case 'DownedPlantera': return downed(ctx, 262);
122	    case 'FirstTimeKillingPlantera': return !downed(ctx, 262); // 掉落先于 downed 写入 → 首杀语义
123	    case 'DownedAllMechBosses': return downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
124	    case 'BeatAnyMechBoss': return downed(ctx, 134) || downed(ctx, 125) || downed(ctx, 127);
125	    case 'MechdusaKill': return false;
126	    case 'FromCertainWaveAndAbove': {
127	      // Conditions.cs:249-261：NPC.waveNumber >= neededWave（不查事件——无事件时
128	      // waveNumber=0 恒不满足；注册仅在冰女王专属掉落 1914 上，neededWave=15）
129	      return (ctx.moonEvent?.wave ?? 0) >= Number(args[0] ?? 0);
130	    }
131	    case 'PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance': case 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance': {
132	      // 稀有掉落总门（Conditions.cs:55-121）：霜月基数 28 / 南瓜月 24；
133	      // 专家 wave+5；denom = ⌊(base-wave)/2.5⌋，专家再 -2(霜)/-1(南瓜)；下限 1；
134	      // RollLuck(denom)==0 才放行（luck=0 ≡ rng.Next(denom)==0）
135	      const me = ctx.moonEvent;
136	      const frost = name === 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance';
137	      if (!me || me.kind !== (frost ? 1 : 2)) return false;
138	      let wave = me.wave;
139	      if (ctx.isExpert) wave += 5;
140	      let denom = Math.trunc(((frost ? 28 : 24) - wave) / 2.5);
141	      if (ctx.isExpert) denom -= frost ? 2 : 1;
142	      if (denom < 1) denom = 1;
143	      return rollLuck(ctx, denom, 1);
144	    }
145	    case 'PumpkinMoonDropGateForTrophies': case 'FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies': {
146	      // 纪念碑门（Conditions.cs:123-229）：wave≥15；denom = 4(15/16)/3(17/18)/2(19/20+)，
147	      // 专家 1/3 概率再 -1；info.rng.Next(denom)==0
148	      const me = ctx.moonEvent;
149	      const frost = name === 'FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies';
150	      if (!me || me.kind !== (frost ? 1 : 2) || me.wave < 15) return false;
151	      let denom = me.wave >= 19 ? 2 : me.wave >= 17 ? 3 : 4;
152	      if (ctx.isExpert && ctx.rng.int(0, 2) === 0) denom--;
153	      return ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, denom) - 1) === 0;
154	    }
155	    case 'EmpressOfLightIsGenuinelyEnraged': return false;
156	    case 'MissingTwin': return false;
157	    case 'RedHatSkeletron': return false;
158	    case 'SkyblockIsUp': case 'SkyblockIsUpNoSickle': return false;
159	    case 'WindyEnoughForKiteDrops': return !!ex?.windy;
160	    case 'EyeOfCthulhuDefeatedAndNoAltarsInWorld': return false; // 天空盒世界专用
161	    case 'NamedNPC': return false; // 具名 NPC 未实现
162	    case 'LivingFlames':
163	      // Conditions.cs:939 lifeMax>5 && value>0 && !friendly && hardMode && y>UnderworldLayer
164	      return ctx.lifeMax > 5 && ctx.value > 0 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.hardMode
165	        && ctx.y / 16 > ctx.underworldTileY;
166	    case 'SoulOfLight': case 'SoulOfNight': {
167	      // SoulOfWhateverConditionCanDrop(Conditions.cs:1574)：hardMode && lifeMax>1 &&
168	      // !friendly && !boss && y>rockLayer && value>=1，排除史莱姆/鸟妖族等
169	      if (!ctx.hardMode || ctx.lifeMax <= 1 || ctx.friendly || ctx.boss || ctx.value < 1) return false;
170	      if (ctx.y / 16 <= ctx.rockLayerTile) return false;
171	      if ([1, 13, 14, 15, 121, 535].includes(ctx.type)) return false;
172	      return name === 'SoulOfLight' ? !!ex?.zoneHallow
173	        : !!(ex?.zoneCorrupt || ex?.zoneCrimson);
174	    }
175	    case 'PirateMap':
176	      // Conditions.cs:391 value>0 && hardMode && y<worldSurface+10 && 近海（x<380 或 >maxTilesX-380）
177	      return ctx.value > 0 && ctx.hardMode && ctx.y / 16 < ctx.worldSurfaceTile + 10
178	        && (ctx.x / 16 < 380 || ctx.x / 16 > ctx.maxTilesX - 380);
179	    case 'JungleKeyCondition': case 'CorruptKeyCondition': case 'CrimsonKeyCondition':
180	    case 'HallowKeyCondition': case 'FrozenKeyCondition': case 'DesertKeyCondition': {
181	      // Conditions.cs:986-1120：value>0 && hardMode && 玩家在对应 zone（Desert 还需非海滩）
182	      if (ctx.value <= 0 || !ctx.hardMode) return false;
183	      switch (name) {
184	        case 'JungleKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneJungle;
185	        case 'HallowKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneHallow;
186	        case 'CorruptKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneCorrupt;
187	        case 'CrimsonKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneCrimson;
188	        case 'FrozenKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneSnow;
189	        case 'DesertKeyCondition': return !!ex?.zoneDesert; // zoneBeach 未入 ctx（近似）
190	      }
191	      return false;
192	    }
193	    case 'YoyoCascade': // Conditions.cs:1136 !hardMode && lifeMax>5 && !friendly && value>0 && y>maxTilesY-350 && downedBoss3
194	      return !ctx.hardMode && ctx.lifeMax > 5 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.value > 0
195	        && ctx.y / 16 > ctx.maxTilesY - 350 && downed(ctx, 35);
196	    case 'YoyosAmarok': // hardMode && ZoneSnow && lifeMax>5 && !friendly && value>0
197	      return ctx.hardMode && !!ex?.zoneSnow && ctx.lifeMax > 5 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.value > 0;
198	    case 'YoyosYelets': // hardMode && ZoneJungle && downedMechAny && …
199	      return ctx.hardMode && !!ex?.zoneJungle && (downed(ctx, 134) || downed(ctx, 125) || downed(ctx, 127))
200	        && ctx.lifeMax > 5 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.value > 0;
201	    case 'YoyosKraken': // hardMode && ZoneDungeon && downedPlantera && …
202	      return ctx.hardMode && !!ex?.zoneDungeon && downed(ctx, 262)
203	        && ctx.lifeMax > 5 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.value > 0;
204	    case 'YoyosHelFire': // hardMode && !ZoneDungeon && y>(rockLayer+maxTilesY*2)/3 && …
205	      return ctx.hardMode && !ex?.zoneDungeon
206	        && ctx.y / 16 > (ctx.rockLayerTile + ctx.maxTilesY * 2) / 3
207	        && ctx.lifeMax > 5 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.value > 0;
208	    default:
209	      warnOnce(`cond:${name}`, `未知掉落条件 ${name}（按 false 处理）`);
210	      return false;
211	  }
212	}
213	
214	// ============ 规则求值（ItemDropResolver.cs 语义） ============
215	/** Player.RollLuck(denom, luck=0) ≡ rng.Next(denom) */
216	function rollLuck(ctx: NpcDropCtx, denom: number, num: number): boolean {
217	  if (denom <= 1) return true;
218	  return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
219	}
220	
221	function pushDrop(out: NpcDropOutcome, id: number, n: number) {
222	  if (n > 0) out.drops.push({ id, n });
223	}
224	
225	function condPass(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean | null {
226	  if (!rule.cond) return true;
227	  const pass = evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx);
228	  return pass;
229	}
230	
231	function evalRule(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx, out: NpcDropOutcome): State {
232	  let state: State;
233	  switch (rule.k) {
234	    case 'common': {
235	      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
236	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
237	        state = 'success';
238	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
239	      break;
240	    }
241	    case 'rerolls': {
242	      // CommonDropWithRerolls：rerolls+1 次掷骰任一成功
243	      let hit = false;
244	      for (let i = 0; i <= (rule.rerolls ?? 0); i++) hit = hit || rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1);
245	      if (hit) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1)); state = 'success'; }
246	      else state = 'failedRoll';
247	      break;
248	    }
249	    case 'cond': {
250	      // ItemDropWithConditionRule：条件不过 = DoesntFillConditions（failedRoll 链不进）
251	      if (!condPass(rule, ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
252	      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
253	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
254	        state = 'success';
255	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
256	      break;
257	    }
258	    case 'oneOf': {
259	      // OneFromOptionsDropRule：过掷后等权取一
260	      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom, rule.num ?? 1)) {
261	        pushDrop(out, rule.ids[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.ids.length - 1)], 1);
262	        state = 'success';
263	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
264	      break;
265	    }
266	    case 'expert': { // DropBasedOnExpertMode：normal/expert 二选一委托
267	      state = evalRule(ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.normal, ctx, out);
268	      break;
269	    }
270	    case 'master': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode
271	      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : rule.def, ctx, out);
272	      break;
273	    }
274	    case 'masterExpert': { // DropBasedOnMasterAndExpertMode：master>expert>def
275	      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.def, ctx, out);
276	      break;
277	    }
278	    case 'bossBag': {
279	      // BossBag = DropBasedOnExpertMode(DropNothing, local(bag)+value=0)；
280	      // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions（非专家分支不进 OnSuccess 链）
281	      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
282	      if (ctx.isExpert) {
283	        // local 规则 rng.Next(1)<1 恒过 → 掉袋 + 清 value
284	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
285	        out.zeroValue = true;
286	        state = 'success';
287	      } else state = 'failedCond';
288	      break;
289	    }
290	    case 'masterCommon': { // ByCondition(IsMasterMode, item)（非 master = DoesntFillConditions）
291	      if (ctx.isMaster) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1); state = 'success'; }
292	      else state = 'failedCond';
293	      break;
294	    }
295	    case 'masterAll': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode(DropNothing, perPlayer(item, denom, IsMasterMode))
296	      if (ctx.isMaster) {
297	        // perPlayer 规则 TryDropping 恒 Success，掷骰在掉落内部决定
298	        if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, 1)) pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
299	        state = 'success';
300	      } else state = 'failedCond';
301	      break;
302	    }
303	    case 'local': case 'perPlayer': {
304	      // DropLocalPerClientAndResetsNPCMoneyTo0：rng.Next 掷骰 + value 清零（单人化=直接掉）
305	      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
306	      if (ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {
307	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
308	        out.zeroValue = true;
309	        state = 'success';
310	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
311	      break;
312	    }
313	    case 'gate': {
314	      // LeadingConditionRule：CanDrop=cond、DropAttempt 恒 success → 放行 OnSuccess 链
315	      state = condPass(rule, ctx) ? 'success' : 'failedCond';
316	      break;
317	    }
318	    case 'nothing': state = 'failedCond'; break; // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions
319	    case 'mechSpawn': {
320	      // MechBossSpawnersDropRule：value>0 && hardMode && 未三王齐灭 → 各 1/2500
321	      const can = ctx.value > 0 && ctx.hardMode && !downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
322	      if (!can) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
323	      if (!downed(ctx, 134) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 556, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
324	      if (!downed(ctx, 125) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 544, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
325	      if (!downed(ctx, 127) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 557, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
326	      state = 'failedRoll';
327	      break;
328	    }
329	    case 'slimeBody': case 'statueMimic': {
330	      // SlimeBodyItemDropRule/StatueMimicItemDropRule：依赖 npc.ai[1]（雕像携带物品）
331	      // 我们无雕像产怪 → 恒不触发
332	      state = 'failedCond';
333	      break;
334	    }
335	    case 'listRef': { // List<IItemDropRule>{…}（OneFromRulesRule 参数）
336	      state = 'success';
337	      break;
338	    }
339	    default:
340	      warnOnce(`rule:${rule.k}`, `未实现的规则 kind ${rule.k}（跳过）`);
341	      state = 'success';
342	      break;
343	  }
344	  // 链：按父状态门控（Chains.cs TryIfSucceeded/TryIfFailedRandomRoll/TryIfDoesntFillConditions）
345	  for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) {
346	    if ((c.on === 'success' && state === 'success')
347	      || (c.on === 'failedRoll' && state === 'failedRoll')
348	      || (c.on === 'failedConditions' && state === 'failedCond')) {
349	      evalRule(c.rule, ctx, out);
350	    }
351	  }
352	  return state;
353	}
354	
355	/**
356	 * 规则掉落主入口（ItemDropSolver.TryDropping 语义）：
357	 * 全局规则 + 该 netID（回退 type）规则按注册序独立求值。
358	 */
359	export function resolveDrops(ctx: NpcDropCtx): NpcDropOutcome {
360	  const out: NpcDropOutcome = { drops: [], zeroValue: false };
361	  const rules = [
362	    ...globalRules,
363	    ...(rulesByNpc.get(ctx.netId) ?? rulesByNpc.get(ctx.type) ?? []),
364	  ];
365	  for (const r of rules) evalRule(r, ctx, out);
366	  return out;
367	}
368	
369	// ============ 钱币管线（NPCLoot_DropMoney :80412 1:1，luck=0 → 单掷） ============
370	export function rollCoins(value: number, ctx: NpcDropCtx, bloodMoon: boolean): ResolvedDrop[] {
371	  const rng = ctx.rng;
372	  let num = value * (1 + rng.int(-20, 75) * 0.01); // Next(-20,76) → [-20,75]
373	  if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(5, 10) * 0.01;    // 1/2 ×(1.05-1.10)
374	  if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(10, 20) * 0.01;   // 1/4 ×(1.10-1.20)
375	  if (rng.int(0, 7) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(15, 30) * 0.01;   // 1/8 ×(1.15-1.30)
376	  if (rng.int(0, 15) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(20, 40) * 0.01;  // 1/16 ×(1.20-1.40)
377	  if (rng.int(0, 31) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(25, 50) * 0.01;  // 1/32 ×(1.25-1.50)
378	  if (rng.int(0, 63) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(50, 100) * 0.01; // 1/64 ×(1.50-2.00)
379	  if (bloodMoon) num *= 1 + rng.int(0, 100) * 0.01;
380	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
381	  while (Math.floor(num) > 0) {
382	    if (num > 1000000) {
383	      let n = Math.floor(num / 1000000);
384	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
385	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
386	      let rest = n;
387	      while (rest > 999) { rest -= 999; drops.push({ id: 74, n: 999 }); }
388	      num -= 1000000 * n;
389	      if (rest > 0) drops.push({ id: 74, n: rest });
390	    } else if (num > 10000) {
391	      let n = Math.floor(num / 10000);
392	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
393	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
394	      num -= 10000 * n;
395	      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 73, n });
396	    } else if (num > 100) {
397	      let n = Math.floor(num / 100);
398	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
399	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
400	      num -= 100 * n;
401	      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 72, n });
402	    } else {
403	      let n = Math.floor(num);
404	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
405	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 3) + 1));
406	      n = Math.max(1, n);
407	      num -= n;
408	      drops.push({ id: 71, n });
409	    }
410	  }
411	  return drops;
412	}
413	
414	// ============ 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana :80332 1:1） ============
415	export function rollHeartsAndStars(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
416	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
417	  if (ctx.type !== 16 && ctx.type !== 81 && ctx.type !== 121
418	    && ctx.lifeMax > 1 && ctx.damage > 0) {
419	    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 5) === 0) { // RollLuck(6)==0
420	      if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 });
421	      else if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.hp < ctx.hpMax) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
422	    }
423	    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 }); // RollLuck(2)==0
424	  }
425	  return drops;
426	}
427	
428	// ============ Boss 药水+心（DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts :79790） ============
429	export function rollBossPotionsAndHearts(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
430	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
431	  const stack = ctx.rng.int(5, 15); // Next(5,16)
432	  let potion = 28; // 弱效治疗药水
433	  if (ctx.type === 113 || ctx.type === 35 || ctx.type === 668) potion = 188; // 治疗药水
434	  else if (ctx.type === 222) potion = 1134;
435	  else if (ctx.type === 657) potion = 499;
436	  else if (ctx.type > 113 && ctx.type < 222) potion = 499; // 强效治疗
437	  else if (ctx.type === 636 || ctx.type === 245 || ctx.type === 262 || ctx.type === 370
438	    || ctx.type === 395 || ctx.type === 439) potion = 499;
439	  else if (ctx.type === 398) potion = 3544;
440	  drops.push({ id: potion, n: stack });
441	  const hearts = ctx.rng.int(5, 9); // Next(5)+5 → 5..9? 原版 Next(5)+5 = 5..9
442	  for (let i = 0; i < hearts; i++) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
443	  return drops;
444	}
445	
446	// ============ 掉落初速度（Item.cs:49325-49337） ============
447	export function dropVelocity(rng: RNG): { vx: number; vy: number } {
448	  return { vx: rng.int(-30, 30) * 0.1, vy: rng.int(-40, -15) * 0.1 };
449	}
450	
451	export { SLIME_BODY };
452

158	/** 按玩家环境取生成池 id 列表。biome：spawn 点地面 tile 判定的群系（Game 传入） */
159	export function poolFor(groundLevel: number, lavaLine: number, ty: number, isDay: boolean, biome?: string): number[] {
160	  if (debugPoolOverride) return debugPoolOverride;
161	  if (ty > lavaLine) return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.hell;
162	  if (ty > groundLevel + 15) return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.underground;
163	  if (biome && VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS[biome as keyof typeof VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS]) {
164	    return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS[biome as keyof typeof VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS] as number[];
165	  }
166	  return isDay ? VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.daySurface : VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.nightSurface;
167	}
168	
169	/** 按生成点地面 tile 判群系（原版 zone 判定的 tile 采样近似） */
170	export function biomeAt(st: { type: Uint16Array; idx(x: number, y: number): number; w: number; h: number }, tx: number, ty: number): string | null {
171	  // 从 ty 向下找第一个实心格
172	  for (let y = Math.max(2, ty); y < Math.min(st.h - 2, ty + 60); y++) {
173	    const t = st.type[st.idx(tx, y)];
174	    if (t === 0) continue;
175	    const key = TILE_KEY_NAME[t];
176	    if (!key) return null;
177	    if (key.includes('corrupt') || key === 'ebonstone_block' || key.includes('ebonsand')) return 'corruption';
178	    if (key.includes('crimson') || key.includes('crimsand') || key === 'crimstone') return 'crimson';
179	    if (key === 'mud') return 'jungle';
180	    if (key === 'ice' || key === 'snow') return 'snow';
181	    if (key === 'sand' || key === 'sandstone' || key === 'hardened_sand') return 'desert';
182	    return null;
183	  }
184	  return null;
185	}
186

./Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules/DropOneByOne.cs
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules;

public class DropOneByOne : IItemDropRule
{
	public struct Parameters
	{
		public int ChanceNumerator;

		public int ChanceDenominator;

		public int MinimumItemDropsCount;

		public int MaximumItemDropsCount;

		public int MinimumStackPerChunkBase;

		public int MaximumStackPerChunkBase;

		public int BonusMinDropsPerChunkPerPlayer;

		public int BonusMaxDropsPerChunkPerPlayer;

		public float GetPersonalDropRate()
		{
			return (float)ChanceNumerator / (float)ChanceDenominator;
		}
	}

	public int itemId;

	public Parameters parameters;

	public List<IItemDropRuleChainAttempt> ChainedRules { get; private set; }

	public DropOneByOne(int itemId, Parameters parameters)
	{
		ChainedRules = new List<IItemDropRuleChainAttempt>();
		this.parameters = parameters;
		this.itemId = itemId;
	}

	public ItemDropAttemptResult TryDroppingItem(DropAttemptInfo info)
	{
		if (info.player.RollLuck(parameters.ChanceDenominator) < parameters.ChanceNumerator)
		{
			int num = info.rng.Next(parameters.MinimumItemDropsCount, parameters.MaximumItemDropsCount + 1);
			int activePlayersCount = Main.CurrentFrameFlags.ActivePlayersCount;
			int minValue = parameters.MinimumStackPerChunkBase + activePlayersCount * parameters.BonusMinDropsPerChunkPerPlayer;
			int num2 = parameters.MaximumStackPerChunkBase + activePlayersCount * parameters.BonusMaxDropsPerChunkPerPlayer;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				CommonCode.DropItemFromNPC(info.npc, itemId, info.rng.Next(minValue, num2 + 1), scattered: true);
			}
			return new ItemDropAttemptResult
			{
				State = ItemDropAttemptResultState.Success
			};
		}
		return new ItemDropAttemptResult
		{
			State = ItemDropAttemptResultState.FailedRandomRoll
		};
	}

	public void ReportDroprates(List<DropRateInfo> drops, DropRateInfoChainFeed ratesInfo)
	{
		float personalDropRate = parameters.GetPersonalDropRate();
		float dropRate = personalDropRate * ratesInfo.parentDroprateChance;
		drops.Add(new DropRateInfo(itemId, parameters.MinimumItemDropsCount * (parameters.MinimumStackPerChunkBase + parameters.BonusMinDropsPerChunkPerPlayer), parameters.MaximumItemDropsCount * (parameters.MaximumStackPerChunkBase + parameters.BonusMaxDropsPerChunkPerPlayer), dropRate, ratesInfo.conditions));
		Chains.ReportDroprates(ChainedRules, personalDropRate, drops, ratesInfo);
	}

	public bool CanDrop(DropAttemptInfo info)
	{
		return true;
	}
}

```

const lerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number) => a + (b - a) * t;
/** 标量区间平滑(驱动向量 SmoothStep 的 amount 因子) */
const smoothAmt = (t: number) => {
  t = clamp(t, 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
};
/** 原版 Vector2.SmoothStep(cur, target, amount):Lerp(amount²(3-2amount)) */
const vSmoothStep = (cur: number, target: number, amount: number) => lerp(cur, target, smoothAmt(amount));
/** 角度插值(原版 float.AngleLerp:取最短方向步进) */
const angleLerp = (cur: number, target: number, t: number) => {
  let d = target - cur;
  while (d > Math.PI) d -= TAU;
  while (d < -Math.PI) d += TAU;
  return cur + d * t;
};

/** 原版 SimpleFlyMovement(:78100)+ 速度折半混合(月总 8/0.5+Lerp0.5 的组合写法):

const flyLerp = (e: Enemy, tx: number, ty: number, acc: number) => {
  const ox = e.vx, oy = e.vy;
  e.vx = accelTo(e.vx, tx, acc);
  e.vy = accelTo(e.vy, ty, acc);
  e.vx = lerp(ox, e.vx, 0.5);
  e.vy = lerp(oy, e.vy, 0.5);
};

```
    // 原版在进入冲撞时一次性应用——每 tick 重乘会把伤害放大 1.3^400,故缓存基值按基值设置
    if (e.ai2 >= 400) { e.ai2 = 0; e.ai1 = 0; }
    let base = baseStats.get(e);
    if (!base) { base = baseOf(e); }
    e.def.defense = Math.max(0, base.def - 10);
    e.def.damage = Math.round(base.dmg * 1.3);
    e.spin += (e.facing || 1) * 0.3;
    const dx = player.cx - e.cx, dy = player.cy - e.cy;
    const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const spd = 12; // (1.5x 追击速)
    e.vx += ((dx / d) * spd - e.vx) * 0.1;
    e.vy += ((dy / d) * spd - e.vy) * 0.1;
  }
  e.facing = e.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  e.x += e.vx; e.y += e.vy;
}

/** Boss 伤害/防御基值缓存(状态机每 tick 从基值派生临时修正,防重复乘膨胀) */
const baseStats = new WeakMap<Enemy, { dmg: number; def: number }>();
const baseOf = (e: Enemy) => {
  let b = baseStats.get(e);
  if (!b) { b = { dmg: e.def.damage, def: e.def.defense ?? 0 }; baseStats.set(e, b); }
  return b;
};

let enemyCtor: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy'] | null = null;
/** Enemy 构造注入(Enemy.ts import 时回填,避免循环依赖) */
export function bindEnemyCtor(c: (typeof import('./Enemy'))['Enemy']) { enemyCtor = c; }

export function spawnPart(game: GameHooks, src: Enemy, id: number): Enemy | null {
  if (!enemyCtor) return null;
  const p = enemyCtor.fromVanilla(id, src.cx, src.cy);
  if (!p) return null;
  return addEnemy(game, p);
}

```

const addProj = (game: GameHooks, p: MagicProj) => {
  (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(p, 'projectiles');
};
export const addEnemy = (game: GameHooks, e: Enemy) => {
  const em = (game as unknown as { entities?: { nextId?: number; add?: (en: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
  if (em?.nextId !== undefined) e.id = em.nextId++;
  em?.add?.(e, 'enemies');
  return e;
};

/** AI_011 骷髅王战斗状态机(NPC.cs:21904-22288 完整核;守卫分支已在 skeletronHeadAI)。

// 确定性随机数：Terraria 1.4.5.6 位级对齐。
//
// ── UnifiedRandom（Terraria.Utilities/UnifiedRandom.cs 1:1）─────────────────
// .NET System.Random 的 Knuth 减法生成器（MSEED=161803398），纯 int32 运算。
// 位级移植要点（JS/C# 数值差异全部钉死，改动前先读）：
//  1. C# int 溢出 unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS：本实现所有中间值都落在 int32 范围内
//     （diff 最小 -(2^31-1)，`+((n>>31)&0x7FFFFFFF)` 归位后 ∈[0,2^31-1]），
//     无需模拟回绕；存储用 Int32Array 与 C# int[] 同构。
//  2. `(int)double` 是向零截断 ↔ JS Math.trunc（Sample()≥0 时与 floor 等价，
//     但必须写 trunc 以防有人把 internalSample 改出负值）。
//  3. `Sample()` 常数 4.656612875245797E-10 = 1/(2^31-1)，十进制字面量两语言
//     解析为同一 double，乘法 IEEE754 位级一致。
//  4. `Next(min,max)` 的 range 用 long（≤2^32）↔ JS number 精确整数，安全。
//     大 range 分支的 `(int)(long+min)` unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS `|0`（ToInt32 mod 2^32）。
//  5. `GetSampleForLargeRange` 的 `% 2 == 0`：C# 余数带符号但只判零，JS `%` 同判。
//  金标：tests/golden/unified-random.json 由真·Terraria.exe 反射导出
//  （tools/golden/rand-golden.cs），tests/unified-random.test.ts 位级断言。
//
// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Math.Abs(int)（int.MinValue→int.MaxValue）；非数字 →
// ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32.Calculate（IEEE 表，按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符，
// 注意 `(crc ^ c) & 0xFF` 只取 char 低字节）。Crc32 结果可为负，直接作种子。
export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  /** C# SetSeed（UnifiedRandom.cs:25-61）1:1 */
  setSeed(seed: number): void {
    this.seedArray.fill(0);
    // C#: (Seed == int.MinValue) ? int.MaxValue : Math.Abs(Seed)
    const num = seed === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(seed);
    let num2 = 161803398 - num;
    this.seedArray[55] = num2;
    let num3 = 1;
    for (let j = 1; j < 55; j++) {
      const num4 = (21 * j) % 55;
      this.seedArray[num4] = num3;
      num3 = (num2 - num3) | 0; // C# unchecked int：可能回绕，|0 = ToInt32 同语义
      if (num3 < 0) num3 += 2147483647; // C# += int.MaxValue（不是 2^32！）
      num2 = this.seedArray[num4];
    }
    for (let k = 1; k < 5; k++) {
      for (let l = 1; l < 56; l++) {
        this.seedArray[l] -= this.seedArray[1 + ((l + 30) % 55)];
        if (this.seedArray[l] < 0) this.seedArray[l] += 2147483647;
      }
    }
    this.inext = 0;
  }

  /** C# Sample（:63-65）*/
  private sample(): number {
    return this.internalSample() * 4.656612875245797e-10;
  }

updateLunarApocalypse(w, has, host);\n  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);\n}\n\n/** ---- 扣盾归属表（NPC.cs:80080-80121：本组怪死亡 → 629 → 对应塔）----\n *  日耀 412/413/414/415/416/417/418/419/518 → 517；星旋 425/426/427/429 → 422；\n *  星云 420/421/423/424 → 507；星尘 402/405/407/409/411 → 493。\n *  （406/408/410/413/414/416/428 是分裂/伴生怪，不扣盾——不在表内即不扣。） */\nexport const MINION_TOWER: Record<number, TowerKey> = (() => {\n  const m: Record<number, TowerKey> = {};\n  for (const id of [412, 413, 414, 415, 416, 417, 418, 419, 518]) m[id] = 'solar';\n  for (const id of [425, 426, 427, 429]) m[id] = 'vortex';\n  for (const id of [420, 421, 423, 424]) m[id] = 'nebula';\n  for (const id of [402, 405, 407, 409, 411]) m[id] = 'stardust';\n  return m;\n})();\n\n/** 击杀怪是否扣盾（发 629 的门：对应塔盾 >0，NPC.cs:80089 等） */\nexport function isTowerMinion(vanillaId: number | null | undefined): boolean {\n  return vanillaId != null && vanillaId in MINION_TOWER;\n}\n\n/** 本组怪死亡 → 扣盾 1（Projectile.cs:69784-69819 命中段 1:1）。\n *  返回被扣盾的塔 id（调用方负责视觉：塔 ai3=1 闪光 + 红色追踪弹尾迹）；\n *  flash = 扣盾前盾值非 0（原版命中时 shield!=0 才置塔 ai[3]=1）；null = 不扣（盾已空/非本组怪） */\nexport function onMinionKilled(w: World, vanillaId: number): { tower: number; flash: boolean } | null {\n  const key = MINION_TOWER[vanillaId];\n  if (!key) return null;\n  const s = w.lunarEvent;\n  const before = s.shield[key];\n  if (before <= 0) return null;   // 原版发弹门：ShieldStrengthTower* > 0 才发 629\n  s.shield[key] = Math.max(0, Math.min(shieldMax(w), before - 1));\n  const towerId = TOWER_IDS.find((t) => TOWER_KEY[t] === key)!;\n  return { tower: towerId, flash: true };\n}\n\n/** SceneMetrics.NPCEventZoneRadius（SceneMetrics.cs:31）= 250 格 */\nexport const TOWER_ZONE_RADIUS = 4000;\n\n/** 月总倒计时递减（Main.cs:64452-64459）：>0 每 tick -1，归零由调用方召 398。\n *  返回 true = 本 tick 归零（调用方立即 SpawnOnPlayer(最近玩家, 398)） */\nexport function tickMoonLordCountdown(w: World): boolean {\n  const s = w.lunarEvent;\n  if (s.moonLordCountdown <= 0) return false;\n  s.moonLordCountdown--;\n  return s.moonLordCountdown <= 0;\n}\n\nvoid TILE;\n"
}

So direction = (0,-1) rotated by θ∈[-π/4,π/4] → up and sideways. Implement: θ=(Math.random()*2-1)*0.785; dir=(sinθ... rotation of (0,-1) by θ: (x', y') = (x cosθ - y sinθ, x sinθ + y cosθ) = (sinθ, -cosθ). times speed.

Movement integration at the end: `this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;` — but note vx is 0 and vy small. But we also did the ground-adherence position tweaks. Order in source: velocity set → ground adherence modifies position directly → bounds clamp → ai0++ → per-tower branches. Position integration happens in the engine (NPC velocity applied elsewhere). In our repo, the AI must integrate. So integrate at the end: this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy. Set onGround=false etc.

Also `this.onGround = false;` to avoid weirdness.

Player-null handling: TargetClosest(faceTarget:false) — with dead player, target may be invalid; source then uses Main.player[target].active/!dead gates in branches. We use `player && !player.dead`.

Now dispatch cases for the approximate families:

```ts
case 94: this.towerAI(game, p); break;
// ---- 星璇塔四组专属怪 AI 一期近似（docs/event-lunar-pillars.md §4；二期 1:1 补齐）----
// 74 穿心者（悬浮俯冲）→ AI_002 飘浮眼核；75 日耀骑手 → AI_003 战士核；
// 85 星细胞/星云头蟹（漂浮吸附）→ AI_005 蜂群核；95 小星细胞 → AI_005；
// 96 水流入侵者（漂浮漂移）→ AI_005；97 星云悬浮脑（瞬移+弹幕）→ AI_008 法师核；
// 99 日耀黏液（塔顶斜抛飞行体）→ AI_005
case 74: this.floatEyeAI(game, p); break;
case 75: this.fighterAI(game, p); break;
case 85: case 95: case 96: case 99: this.swarmerAI(game, p); break;
case 97: this.casterAI(game, p); break;
```

 "new_string": "  /** AI_094 星璇四塔（NPC.cs:41029-41443，1:1）。塔 = NPC（非 tile entity）。\n   *  原版 dontTakeDamage（盾>0 完全免伤，:41162-41176 每 tick 重算）用 iframes=2 每 tick\n   *  刷新近似（同 bossAI 克脑一阶段先例）。血尽先由 hurt() 转 ai2=1 死亡演出（checkDead\n   *  :78867-78875），180t 渐隐后走标准死亡管线（碎片掉落/塔灭公告）。\n   *  分塔支线（493 星尘标记/422 星旋传送门/517 日耀耀斑）按文档简化许可折叠为\n   *  \"直接在标记点 spawnNPC\"（540/578/579 传送门演出二期补） */\n  private towerAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n    const w = game.world;\n    const key = TOWER_KEY[this.vanillaId ?? -1];\n    if (!key) return;\n    const le = w.lunarEvent;\n    const st = w.store;\n    const count = (id: number): number => {\n      let n = 0;\n      for (const o of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {\n        if (!o.dead && o.vanillaId === id) n++;\n      }\n      return n;\n    };\n    const distTo = (px: number, py: number) => Math.hypot(px - this.cx, py - this.cy);\n\n    // ---- 1. 死亡演出（ai2==1，:41031-41128）----\n    if (this.ai2 === 1) {\n      // velocity = UnitY * velocity.Length()（:41033）：保留速率只留 +Y 分量，±0.25 钳（步进 0.02）\n      const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);\n      this.vx = 0;\n      this.vy = spd;\n      if (this.vy < 0.25) this.vy += 0.02;\n      if (this.vy > 0.25) this.vy -= 0.02;\n      this.iframes = 2;                                  // dontTakeDamage = true（:41042）\n      this.ai1++;\n      if (this.ai1 > 120) {\n        // Opacity = 1-(ai1-120)/60（:41046）→ 本仓 spawnAlpha（0..255，越大越透明）\n        const opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - (this.ai1 - 120) / 60);\n        this.spawnAlpha = Math.round((1 - opacity) * 255);\n      }\n      // 分塔爆散尘（:41048-41117：三组粒子 + 241 白尘；此处按塔色近似两组）\n      const dustCol = this.vanillaId === 517 ? '#FFB060' : this.vanillaId === 422 ? '#8CE8C8'\n        : this.vanillaId === 507 ? '#E070E0' : '#7CB8FF';\n      if (Math.random() < 0.2 && this.ai1 < 120) {\n        game.spawnParticles(this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.w, this.cy, dustCol, 3, 1.2,\n          { life: 40, grav: -0.02 });\n      }\n      if (Math.random() < 0.2 && this.ai1 < 150) {\n        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y - 20, dustCol, 6, 2.4, { life: 40, grav: -0.05 });\n        game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#F0F0F0', 1, 0.8, { life: 30, grav: 0.02 });\n      }\n      // 每 60t 音效（:41118-41121 SoundID 4/22 碎裂；音效未接 → 低响咆哮近似）\n      if (this.ai1 % 60 === 1) game.playSfx('roar', 0.3, this.cx, this.cy);\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n      if (this.ai1 >= 180) {\n        // life=0 + HitEffect + checkDead（:41122-41127）→ 真死（碎片 3456-3459 + 塔灭公告）\n        this.iframes = 0;\n        this.hurt(1e9, 0, 0, game);\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 2. 受击闪光 ai3>0（:41130-41161；ai3 由扣盾置 1，>120 归零）----\n    if (this.ai3 > 0) {\n      if (this.ai3 === 1) game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_3'], 1, this.cx, this.cy); // :41152-41155\n      this.ai3++;\n      if (this.ai3 > 120) this.ai3 = 0;\n    }\n    // ---- 3. 盾判定（:41162-41176）：dontTakeDamage = 本塔 ShieldStrength>0（每帧重算）----\n    // （盾破瞬间的 NPCDeath58 音效由 Game.onEnemyKilled 扣盾归零处播放——等价 :41148-41151）\n    if (le.shield[key] > 0) this.iframes = 2;\n\n    // ---- 4. 远离自愈（:41177-41191）----\n    if (player && distTo(player.cx, player.cy) > 2000) this.lai0++;\n    if (this.lai0 >= 60) {\n      this.lai0 = 0;\n      this.hp = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.maxHp, this.hp + 200));\n    } else {\n      this.lai0 = 0;   // 原版 else 每 tick 清零（:41188-41191）——自愈实际不可达，照抄不\"修\"\n    }\n\n    // ---- 5. 悬停（:41192）+ 贴地（:41193-41214）----\n    this.vx = 0;\n    this.vy = Math.sin(Math.PI * 2 * (this.ai0 / 300)) * 0.5;\n    const ox = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);\n    const oy = Math.floor((this.y + this.h) / TILE);\n    const solidDown = (maxDist: number): number => {\n      for (let d = 0; d <= maxDist; d++) {\n        const yy = oy + d;\n        if (!st.inBounds(ox, yy)) return -1;\n        if (st.isSolid(ox, yy)) return yy;\n      }\n      return -1;\n    };\n    const near = solidDown(10);\n    if (near >= 0) {\n      // 近（≤10 格）：按距离比下沉 1.5×（:41200-41204，塔底压向地面）\n      this.y -= 1.5 * (1 - Math.abs(oy - near) / 10);\n    } else if (solidDown(20) < 0) {\n      // 远（>20 格）：上浮 1.5×，20-30 格间线性插值（:41205-41213）\n      let f = 1;\n      const far = solidDown(30);\n      if (far >= 0) f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (Math.abs(oy - far) - 20) / 10));\n      this.y += 1.5 * f;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 6. 世界边界钳制（:41215-41238；60 格边距 + 80px 内推）----\n    // 原版 Y 上限也取 maxTilesX*16（:41215-41216 的 num1489 怪癖）——照抄\n    const limX = w.w * TILE;\n    const limY = w.w * TILE;\n    const m = 60 * TILE;\n    if (this.cx < m) this.x = m + 80 - this.w / 2;\n    if (this.cx > limX - m) this.x = limX - m - 80 - this.w / 2;\n    if (this.cy < m) this.y = m + 80 - this.h / 2;\n    if (this.cy > limY - m) this.y = limY - m - 80 - this.h / 2;\n    // 普通世界塔底 ≤ worldSurface*16-100（:41247-41250）\n    if (this.y + this.h > w.groundLevel * TILE - 100) {\n      this.y = w.groundLevel * TILE - this.h - 100;\n    }\n\n    this.ai0++;\n    if (this.ai0 >= 300) this.ai0 = 0;   // :41251-41256\n\n    // ---- 7. 分塔支线 ----\n    if (this.vanillaId === 493) {\n      // 星尘（:41257-41286）：环境尘略；SpawnStardustMark_StardustTower（:44142-44228）\n      if (this.ai1 > 0) this.ai1--;\n      if (this.ai1 <= 0 && player && !player.dead\n        && distTo(player.cx, player.cy) < 1080 && player.y - this.y < 400) {\n        const pool: number[] = [];\n        if (count(405) + count(406) < 2) pool.push(405);\n        if (count(402) < 2) pool.push(402);\n        if (count(407) < 1) pool.push(407);\n        if (pool.length > 0) {\n          const pick = pool[(Math.random() * pool.length) | 0];\n          this.ai1 = 30 * (5 + ((Math.random() * 11) | 0));   // 30*Next(5,16)\n          // 投射物 540 星尘标记 → 末端生成该 NPC：折叠为塔顶随机点直接 spawn（无飞行演出）\n          const mark = spawnPart(game, this, pick);\n          if (mark) {\n            mark.x = this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 160 - mark.w / 2;\n            mark.y = this.y - 120 - mark.h / 2;\n          }\n        } else {\n          this.ai1 = 30;   // :44225-44227\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (this.vanillaId === 422) {\n      // 星旋（:41310-41407）：玩家头顶传送门 + 空中随机点传送门\n      if (this.ai1 > 0) this.ai1--;\n      // 支线 a（:41336-41361）：3240px 内（原版还要求 !CanHitLine 视线阻隔——未接，省略）→\n      // 玩家头顶门；n428+n427+n426<14 → 579 门（90t 后生 2× Alien Hornet 427）否则 578 门（只放电）\n      if (this.ai1 <= 0 && player && !player.dead && distTo(player.cx, player.cy) < 3240) {\n        this.ai1 = 60 + ((Math.random() * 120) | 0);\n        if (count(428) + count(427) + count(426) < 14) {\n          const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE);\n          let py = Math.floor(player.y / TILE);\n          for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {\n            if (!st.inBounds(ptx, py) || st.isSolid(ptx, py) || py <= 10) break;\n            py--;\n          }\n          for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            const hornet = spawnPart(game, this, 427);\n            if (!hornet) continue;\n            hornet.x = ptx * TILE + 8 - hornet.w / 2;\n            hornet.y = py * TILE + 24 - hornet.h;\n            const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n            const sp = 4 + Math.random() * 5;               // Next(4,9)\n            hornet.vx = Math.cos(a) * sp;\n            hornet.vy = Math.sin(a) * sp - 2;               // - Vector2.UnitY*2\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      // 支线 b（:41362-41406）：1080px 内且低于塔顶 400px、n427+n426*3+n428<20 →\n      // 玩家 ±20 格随机空中点 579 门（2× 427）\n      if (this.ai1 <= 0 && player && !player.dead\n        && distTo(player.cx, player.cy) < 1080 && player.y - this.y < 400\n        && count(427) + count(426) * 3 + count(428) < 20) {\n        this.ai1 = 420 + ((Math.random() * 360) | 0);\n        const ptx = Math.floor(player.cx / TILE);\n        const pty = Math.floor(player.cy / TILE);\n        const ttx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE);\n        const tty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);\n        for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 100; attempt++) {\n          const mx = ptx - 20 + ((Math.random() * 41) | 0);\n          const my = pty - 20 + ((Math.random() * 41) | 0);\n          if (Math.abs(my - pty) <= 8 && Math.abs(mx - ptx) <= 8) continue;   // :41383 玩家 8 格禁圈\n          if (Math.abs(my - tty) <= 3 && Math.abs(mx - ttx) <= 3) continue;   // :41383 塔 3 格禁圈\n          if (!st.inBounds(mx, my) || st.isSolid(mx, my)) continue;\n          for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            const hornet = spawnPart(game, this, 427);\n            if (!hornet) continue;\n            hornet.x = mx * TILE + 8 - hornet.w / 2;\n            hornet.y = my * TILE + 8 - hornet.h / 2;\n            const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n            const sp = 4 + Math.random() * 5;\n            hornet.vx = Math.cos(a) * sp;\n            hornet.vy = Math.sin(a) * sp - 2;\n          }\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n    } else if (this.vanillaId === 517) {\n      // 日耀（:41431-41443）：1080px 内且低于塔顶 700px → 塔顶直投 519（斜抛 7-12px/t）\n      if (this.ai1 > 0) this.ai1--;\n      if (this.ai1 <= 0 && player && !player.dead\n        && distTo(player.cx, player.cy) < 1080 && player.y - this.y < 700) {\n        const flare = spawnPart(game, this, 519);\n        if (flare) {\n          flare.x = this.x - this.w * 0.33 + Math.random() * this.w * 0.66 - flare.w / 2;\n          flare.y = this.y - 20;\n          const theta = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.7853981852531433;   // RotatedByRandom(0.785)\n          const sp = 7 + Math.random() * 5;\n          flare.vx = Math.sin(theta) * sp;      // -UnitY 旋转 θ：(sinθ, -cosθ)\n          flare.vy = -Math.cos(theta) * sp;\n        }\n        this.ai1 = 60;\n      }\n    }\n    // 507 星云仅环境粒子（:41287-41309），怪全靠 Zone 刷怪表——略\n\n    // noTileCollide + noGravity：直接位移（原版速度由引擎积分）\n    this.x += this.vx;\n    this.y += this.vy;\n    this.onGround = false;\n  }\n\n  /** 受击。返回是否死亡 */"
}

e.w / 2;\n    e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n  }\n\n  /** TriggerLunarApocalypse（WorldGen.cs:87371-87436：教徒死调用，见 Boss 逻辑块） */\n  triggerLunarApocalypse(): void {\n    triggerLunarApocalypse(this.world, new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0), this.lunarHost());\n  }\n\n  /** 场上是否存在存活 NPC id（UpdateLunarApocalypse 的塔/月总在场探测用） */\n  private hasNpcId(id: number): boolean {\n    return this.entities.enemies.some((e) => {\n      const en = e as Enemy;\n      return !en.dead && en.vanillaId === id;\n    });\n  }\n\n  /** 塔真死（NPC.cs:80122-80146：downedTower_* + Update/MessageLunarApocalypse） */\n  private onLunarTowerKilled(towerId: number): void {\n    onTowerKilled(this.world, towerId, (id) => this.hasNpcId(id), this.lunarHost());\n  }\n\n  /** 本组怪死亡 → 扣对应塔盾 1（LunarEvent.onMinionKilled = Projectile.cs:69784-69819 命中段）\n   *  + 视觉：塔 ai3=1 闪光、629 红色追踪弹（此处粒子尾迹近似，二期接真弹体） */\n  private onLunarMinionKilled(enemy: Enemy): void {\n    const hit = onMinionKilled(this.world, enemy.vanillaId!);\n    if (!hit) return;\n    const tower = (this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]).find(\n      (e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === hit.tower);\n    if (!tower) return;\n    tower.ai3 = 1;   // Projectile.cs:69791 等：命中且盾非 0 → 塔 ai[3]=1（闪光 120t）\n    // 629 TowerDamageBolt（5px/t 追塔 + 红尘尾，Projectile.cs:69784 起）：沿路径铺红粒近似\n    const steps = Math.max(4, Math.min(24, Math.floor(Math.hypot(tower.cx - enemy.cx, tower.cy - enemy.cy) / 24)));\n    for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {\n      const t = i / steps;\n      this.spawnParticles(\n        enemy.cx + (tower.cx - enemy.cx) * t + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6,\n        enemy.cy + (tower.cy - enemy.cy) * t + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6,\n        Math.random() < 0.7 ? '#FF4040' : '#FF9060', 1, 0.6, { life: 18 + ((Math.random() * 10) | 0) });\n    }\n    // 盾破瞬间 NPCDeath58（NPC.cs:41148-41151 的 flag99 翻转支）\n    if (this.world.lunarEvent.shield[TOWER_KEY[hit.tower]] === 0) {\n      this.sfx.play('roar', 0.8, tower.cx, tower.cy);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 倒计时归零召月总（Main.cs:64457：SpawnOnPlayer(FindClosest, 398)——单人即主角头顶）。\n   *  入 Boss 槽（专属 BGM 38 / 血条 / 击败记账 downed_398 → shieldMax 减半为 50） */\n  private spawnMoonLordOnPlayer(): void {\n    if (this.boss) return;   // 已有 Boss 在场（原版 SpawnOnPlayer 同样照召；此处避免双 Boss 槽冲突）\n    const p = this.player;\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(398, p.cx, p.cy - 160);\n    if (!e) return;\n    e.x = p.cx - e.w / 2;\n    e.y = p.cy - 160 - e.h / 2;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    this.boss = e;\n  }"
}

// 自有存档格式：JSON 外壳（人可读）+ RLE 二进制 tile 段（base64）
// 序列化纯核心在 ./serialize(无 DOM/World 依赖,主线程与 save.worker.ts 共用同一实现);
// 本文件保留 SaveData 类型/读档解码与主线程同步 saveGame(兼容入口,worker 不可用时回退)。
import type { World } from '../world/World';
import { World as WorldClass } from '../world/World';
import type { Player } from '../entities/Player';
import { serializeSave } from './serialize';
import type { SaveData, SaveStoreView, SaveMeta } from './serialize';
import { TILE_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE } from '../data/tiles';
import { ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE } from '../data/items';
import { weatherToSave } from '../world/weather/Weather';

export { bytesToB64, rlePairs, rleTiles, serializeSave } from './serialize';
export type { SaveData, SaveStoreView, SaveMeta } from './serialize';

function b64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
  const s = atob(b64);
  const u8 = new Uint8Array(s.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u8[i] = s.charCodeAt(i);
  return u8;

Implement helper in Renderer:

```ts
/** 星璇塔护盾半球（Main.cs:23797-23830 1:1 近似）：原版 = Perlin 噪声 600×600 +
 *  ForceField 着色器；此处径向渐变圆顶替代。强度 = 盾/Max（alpha = 0.2+0.8×比），
 *  塔 ai3∈(0,30] 时 +闪光脉冲（强度 +num266、半径 ×(1+num266*0.05)） */
private drawTowerShield(e: Enemy, world: World) {
  const le = world.lunarEvent;
  const key = TOWER_KEY[e.vanillaId ?? -1];
  if (!key) return;
  const max = shieldMax(world);
  const shield = le.shield[key];
  if (shield <= 0) return;
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  const ratio = shield / max;
  let flash = 0;
  if (e.ai3 > 0 && e.ai3 <= 30) flash = 1 - e.ai3 / 30;   // :23803-23806
  const alpha = Math.min(1, ratio * 0.8 + 0.2) * (1 + flash * 0.5);
  const R = 300 * (1 + flash * 0.05);
  const col = TOWER_SHIELD_COLOR[e.vanillaId!];
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(e.cx, e.cy, R * 0.2, e.cx, e.cy, R);
  g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${col},0)`);
  g.addColorStop(0.75, `rgba(${col},${(alpha*0.35).toFixed(3)})`);
  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${col},${(alpha*0.85).toFixed(3)})`);
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.arc(e.cx, e.cy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
}
```

 "new_string": "  /** 星璇塔护盾半球（Main.cs:23797-23830 近似）：原版 = 600×600 Perlin 噪声 +\n   *  ForceField 着色器（分塔 Solar/Vortex/Nebula/Stardust 预设）；此处用径向渐变\n   *  圆顶替代（半径 300px = 原图半宽）。强度 = 盾/ShieldMax（alpha = 0.2+0.8×比），\n   *  塔 ai3∈(0,30] 时闪光脉冲（强度 ×(1+flash*0.5)、半径 ×(1+flash*0.05)）。\n   *  盾空（含死亡演出态）不画；盾破时的碎裂环（:23831+ num267 段）二期补 */\n  private drawTowerShield(e: Enemy, world: World) {\n    const key = TOWER_KEY[e.vanillaId ?? -1];\n    if (!key) return;\n    const shield = world.lunarEvent.shield[key];\n    if (shield <= 0) return;\n    const ratio = shield / shieldMax(world);\n    let flash = 0;\n    if (e.ai3 > 0 && e.ai3 <= 30) flash = 1 - e.ai3 / 30;   // :23803-23806\n    const alpha = Math.min(1, ratio * 0.8 + 0.2) * (1 + flash * 0.5);\n    const R = 300 * (1 + flash * 0.05);\n    const col = TOWER_SHIELD_COLOR[e.vanillaId!];\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(e.cx, e.cy, R * 0.2, e.cx, e.cy, R);\n    g.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${col},0)`);\n    g.addColorStop(0.75, `rgba(${col},${(alpha * 0.35).toFixed(3)})`);\n    g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(${col},${Math.min(1, alpha * 0.85).toFixed(3)})`);\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n    ctx.fillStyle = g;\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(e.cx, e.cy, R, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  /** AI_013 植物/攀爬者族茎蔓（Main.cs:22433-22514 1:1）：从 NPC 中心向锚点格\n   *  中心（ai0/ai1 各 ×16+8）逐段画 Chain 贴图——步长 28px、距锚 <40px 时尾段"
}

```
// 地牢刷怪链验证（Node 直测，不依赖浏览器）：
// 1) findSpawnTile + 地牢落脚拒绝（PostCheck L924）+ SpawnAnNPC 地牢分支（L2536-2706）
// 2) 未杀骷髅王 → 只出守卫 68；杀过 → 常规池
// 3) 出怪实体的 aiStyle/数值正确（fromVanilla 数据路径）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { VanillaSpawner } from '../src/world/spawn/VanillaSpawner';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

async function makeWorld(): Promise<World> {
  return generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'dungeon-spawn-chain' });
}

/** 找地牢内部落脚点：本人格为地牢砖地面(DUNGEON_BLUE/GREEN/PINK，与 spawner
 *  isDungeonTile 同口径) + 本人格有地牢墙(7/8/9/94-99) + 上方 3 格净空 + 地表线以下。
 *  须为干燥点——SpawnAnNPC 水域分支(水池 L1839)在地牢分支(L2536)之前，
 *  水点会出水母 63 而非守卫（原版顺序如此）；生成流任何变动都会平移世界形态，
 *  选点直接要求地牢砖地面（PostCheck L922-927 的落点硬条件），保证稳定命中地牢分支 */
function findDungeonSpot(world: World): { x: number; y: number } | null {
  return findDungeonSpots(world, 1)[0] ?? null;
}

/** 收集最多 max 个候选落脚点（生成流变动会平移地牢形态——单点可能落在
 *  spawner 采样环恒不命中的位置，多候选兜底） */
// 地牢砖 tile(原版 41 蓝/43 绿/44 粉)
const DBLUE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_41_blue_brick'] ?? -1;
const DGREEN = TILE_BY_KEY['v_43_green_brick'] ?? -1;
const DPINK = TILE_BY_KEY['v_44_pink_brick'] ?? -1;

function findDungeonSpots(world: World, max: number): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> {
  const out: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
  const st = world.store;
  const isDW = (w: number) => w === 7 || w === 8 || w === 9 || (w >= 94 && w <= 99);
  const y1 = Math.min(st.h - 10, Math.floor(world.rockLevel) + 100);
  for (let y = Math.max(10, Math.floor(world.groundLevel) + 1); y < y1 && out.length < max; y++) {
    for (let x = 100; x < st.w - 100 && out.length < max; x++) {
      const i = st.idx(x, y);
      if (!isDW(st.wall[i])) continue;
      const t = st.type[i];
      if (t !== DBLUE && t !== DGREEN && t !== DPINK) continue; // 地牢砖地面
      if (!st.isSolid(x, y)) continue;
      if (st.isSolid(x, y - 1) || st.isSolid(x, y - 2) || st.isSolid(x, y - 3)) continue;
      if (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] > 40 || st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 2)] > 40) continue; // 干燥点
      out.push({ x, y });
    }
  }
  return out;
}

describe('地牢刷怪链（VanillaSpawner 地牢分支）', () => {
  it('未杀骷髅王 → 地牢守卫；杀过 → 常规怪池', async () => {
    const world = await makeWorld();
    const spots = findDungeonSpots(world, 12);
    expect(spots.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const spawner = new VanillaSpawner(world);

    // 未杀骷髅王：期望全部为守卫 68（flag12 分支）——多点采样直到出怪
    spawner.setPlayerFlags(true, false);
    const guardianIds = new Set<number>();
    let activeSpot = spots[0];
    for (const spot of spots) {
      guardianIds.clear();
      const playerX = (spot.x + 0.5) * 16;
      const playerY = (spot.y - 3) * 16;
      for (let k = 0; k < 800; k++) {
        const e = spawner.spawn(playerX, playerY, new RNG(k * 7919 + 13), 30, 18);
        if (e) guardianIds.add(e.vanillaId ?? -1);
      }
      if (guardianIds.size > 0) { activeSpot = spot; break; }
    }
    console.log('未杀骷髅王出怪:', [...guardianIds], 'at', activeSpot);
    expect(guardianIds.size).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    // 63=蓝水母：SpawnAnNPC 水域分支(L1839)在地牢分支(L2536)之前——生成点采样环
    // 内有水格时出水母是原版顺序的正确行为（地牢连水洞属正常世界形态）；
    // 核心断言 = 绝不漏常规怪池（31/32/34/70/71/72/294-296）
    for (const id of guardianIds) expect(id === 68 || id === 63).toBe(true);
    expect(guardianIds.has(68)).toBe(true);

```

RNG(1234 + towerId);\n    const ctx: NpcDropCtx = {\n      rng, netId: towerId, type: towerId,\n      isExpert: false, isMaster: false, hardMode: true, crimson: false,\n      flags: world.flags, x: 0, y: 0,\n      worldSurfaceTile: world.groundLevel, rockLayerTile: world.rockLevel,\n      underworldTileY: world.lavaLine, maxTilesX: world.w, maxTilesY: world.h,\n      lifeMax: 20000, friendly: res.drops.filter((d) => d.id === frag);\n    const allFrag = res.drops.every((d) => d.id === frag);\n    const stacksOk = chunks.every((d) => d.n >= 1 && d.n <= 3);\n    check(`塔 ${towerId} 碎片 ${frag}：${chunks.length} 块（12-20）× 每块 1-3`,\n      allFrag && chunks.length >= 12 && chunks.length <= 20 && stacksOk,\n      `drops=${JSON.stringify(res.drops)}`);\n    // 专家档：每块 2-5（基数 ×1.5 + 1 玩家 +1）\n    const ctxE: NpcDropCtx = { ...ctx, isExpert: true, rng: new RNG(999 + towerId) };\n    const resE = resolveDrops(ctxE);\n    const chunksE = resE.drops.filter((d) => d.id === frag);\n    const stacksOkE = chunksE.every((d) => d.n >= 2 && d.n <= 5);\n    check(`塔 ${towerId} 专家碎片：${chunksE.length} 块 × 每块 2-5`,\n      chunksE.length >= 12 && chunksE.length <= 20 && stacksOkE,\n [tower]);\n  world.lunarEvent.active = true;\n  world.lunarEvent.shield.solar = 0;\n  let drops: Array<{ id: number; n: number }> = [];\n  (hooks2 as unknown as { onEnemyKilled: (k: string, e?: Enemy) => void }).onEnemyKilled\n    = (_k, e) => { if (e) drops.push({ id: e.vanillaId ?? 0, n: 1 }); };\n  const survived = tower.hurt(99999, 0, 0, hooks2);\n  check('塔血尽 → 转死亡演出（不直死）', !survived && ## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-11T09:57:40.656Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
export class EntityManager {
  nextId = 1;
  enemies: Entity[] = [];
  drops: Entity[] = [];
  npcs: Entity[] = [];
  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）

  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
    e.id = this.nextId++;
    this[bucket].push(e);
  }

  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
      compact(list);
    }
  }

  clear() {
    this.enemies.length = 0;
    this.drops.length = 0;
    this.npcs.length = 0;
    this.projectiles.length = 0;
    this.props.length = 0;
    this.critters.length = 0;
  }

  all(): Entity[] {
    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
  }
}

```
